## Document 008: 115年10月賦稅收入初步統計 (2026-11-11)

Report source: MOF
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

網際網路 https://www.mof.gov.tw 同步發送 
下次發布日期：115 年 12 月 11 日下午 4 時

聯 絡 人 ： 陳 巧 芸 科 長 
電   話：02-2322-7555 
115年 10月賦稅收入初步統計 
一、整體賦稅收入 
（一）實徵淨額 
10 月實徵淨額 5,669 億元，較上年同月增加 1,495 億元；累計 1 至
10 月實徵 4 兆 1,383 億元，較上年同期增加 8,499 億元(+25.8%)，
主要由證交稅、營所稅及營業稅所貢獻。隨新版財政收支劃分法實
施，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率由 61.2%降為 4.5%(獲配稅額隨之減
少 2,662 億元)，致中央政府稅收增加數 3,906 億元(+15.8%)，不及
地方政府增加數 4,671 億元(+68.5%)。 
（二）預算達成率 
累 計1 至 10 月 實 徵 淨 額 占 全 年 預 算 數 91.4% ， 其 中 中 央 政 府 為
92.8%，地方政府為87.7%；與全年預算數差距分別為3,913億元、
2,227億元、1,614億元。 
圖 1 賦稅實徵淨額及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）10 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營利事業所得稅增加 1,133 億元、
證券交易稅增加 220 億元及綜合所得稅增加 120 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,297
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 2,986 億元及營業稅增加 1,272 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-10 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)10 月實徵淨額 4,144 億元，較上年同月增加 1,133 億元(+37.6%)，
主因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 1 兆 4,172 億元，較上年同期增加 2,986
億元(+26.7%)，主因結算申報自繳、暫繳稅款俱增及延分期稅款
陸續入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
10 月實徵淨額 614 億元，較上年同月增加 120 億元(+24.3%)，主因
扣繳稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 8,894 億元，較上年同
期增加 910 億元(+11.4%)，主因結算申報自繳稅額增加，以及盈餘分

配、薪資所得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
(1)10月受進口代徵稅額增加及適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅增加
之交互影響，營業稅實徵淨額為負77億元，較上年同月(負146億元)
增加69億元。
(2)累 計1 至10 月 實 徵 淨額6,153 億 元 ， 較 上年 同 期 增 加1,272 億元
(+26.1%)，主因內需穩定，資通與視聽產品、電子零組件等進口
代徵稅額增加，惟外銷營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

3
4.貨物稅
10月實徵淨額73億元，較上年同月減少41億元(-35.6%)，累計1至10
月實徵淨額948億元，較上年同期減少248億元(-20.7%)，主因新購汽
機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增加，加以汽柴油減徵幅度擴大所致。
5.證券交易稅
(1)10 月及累計 1 至 10 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 1,210
億元、1 兆 1,437 億元，分別較上年同期(6,267 億元、4,632 億元)
增 78.9%、1.5 倍。
(2)10 月證交稅實徵淨額 523 億元， 較上年同月增加 220 億元
(+72.6%)；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 5,583 億元，較上年同期增加
3,297 億元(+1.4 倍)。
6.關稅
10 月實徵淨額 132 億元，較上年同月增加 7 億元(+6.0%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 1,356 億元，較上年同期增加 88 億元(+6.9%)，均因機
械及電機設備、基本金屬、塑橡膠及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
10 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月減少 11 億元(-31.9%)，主因大額

稅款案件減少；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 234 億元，較上年同期增加
32 億元(+15.9%)，主因大額稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
10 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+5.9%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 10 月實徵淨額 560 億元，較上年同期減少
11 億元(-2.0%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.使用牌照稅
10 月實徵淨額 6 億元，較上年同月減少 1 億元(-10.2%)，累計 1 至
10 月實徵淨額 676 億元，較上年同期減少 1 億元(-0.1%)，主因開徵
輛數減少，以及稅款入帳時間差異所致。
（三）前 10月主要稅目實徵淨額占全年預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%(已提前達成全年預算目標)者有遺產稅(123%)、贈與稅
(107%)、印花稅(106%)、房屋稅(105%)及營所稅(102%)。
2.低於 100%者有牌照稅(99%)、營業稅(94%)、綜所稅(93%)、契稅
(90%)、關稅(88%)、菸酒稅(81%)、證交稅(80%)、貨物稅(79%)、土

增稅(71%)及特銷稅(47%)等。

### Page 4

4
三、政府別賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 10 月中央政府賦稅收入(不含中央統籌分配稅款)實徵淨
額 2 兆 8,579 億元(占全年預算數 92.8%)，較上年同期增加 3,906 億元
(+15.8%)，主因新版財政收支劃分法施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府比率
大幅調降，獲配稅額隨之減少 2,662 億元，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加
3,297 億元、3,306 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 10 月地方政府賦稅
收入(含中央統籌分配稅款)實
徵淨額 1 兆 1,487 億元(占全年
預算數 87.7%)，較上年同期增
加 4,671 億元(+68.5%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配之營業稅
增加 4,013 億元所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 10 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 220 億元，較
上年同期增加 4 億元(+1.7%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 144
億元，較上年同期減少 6 億元(-4.0%)；撥入長照基金 953 億元(其中 575

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、220 億元來自菸稅，158 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.2%)。

本次報告：法說會

儒鴻 (1476 TT) 
不利因素消退，營運展望正向

永豐觀點 
隨著美國期中選舉結果出爐，關稅及戰爭兩大不利因素有望逐步消退，有利成衣產
業評價回升。公司訂單能見度維持六個月，新興品牌客戶訂單動能強勁，推升營收
及獲利表現，後市展望持續正向，研究部上調儒鴻目標價，維持買進投資評等。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進，目標價上調至 500 元：儒鴻 3Q26 毛利率表現優於預期，反
映新興品牌客戶訂單動能強勁，改善產品組合，且原材料成本壓力舒緩，帶動獲利
表現回升。展望後市，儒鴻訂單能見度維持六個月，新興品牌客戶訂單動能持續成
長強勁，既有品牌客戶亦維持穩定回補庫存力道，營運展望正向。研究部預估公司
2026/2027 稅後 EPS 25.39/27.79 元，上調本益比評價至 18X，依 2027 之 EPS
進行評價，目標價上調至 500 元，投資評等維持買進，依公司去年股利配發率
74.63%估算，以昨日收盤價來看，目前約具備 4.9%殖利率，應具備下檔殖利率保
護。

ESG 評析 
儒鴻企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

儒鴻為國內成衣業龍頭廠商：儒鴻成立於 1977 年，主要生產彈性針織布與運動服
飾，並垂直整合染整、織布到成衣製造，具有一條龍整合優勢，此外公司持續提升
布料研發和服飾設計能力，積極研發出具流行性、運動性、休閒性、環保性等功能
性之布料，廣泛應用於高級時裝、運動服、休閒服裝、內睡衣、韻律裝等多樣化產
品系列，並銷售至全球知名品牌及零售商，公司 2025 年營收結構為：成衣 66.4%、
針織布 33.6%，銷售地區以美洲為主，主要客戶包括 Nike、Lululemon、Under 
Armour 等知名大廠。 
3Q26 EPS 6.36 元，符合預期：儒鴻 3Q26 營收 105.72 億元（+3.9% QoQ，
+8.8% YoY），略優於預期，主因新興品牌客戶訂單需求強勁，推升營收表現，毛
利率 31.18%（+1.17pts. QoQ，+2.52pts. YoY），優於預期，除因新興品牌客戶
訂單增長，帶動產品組合改善外，儒鴻持續與客戶進行議價，以轉嫁原材料成本上
漲，推升毛利率表現回升，稅後淨利 17.4 億元（+14.2% QoQ，+7.3% YoY），符
合預期，其中，營業費用高於預期，主因儒鴻獲利強勁，認列季獎金費用較預期高
所致，3Q26 稅後 EPS 6.36 元，符合預期。

10 月營收符合預期，4Q26 營收預估季增：儒鴻 10 月營收 34 億元，-3.1% MoM，
+10.7% YoY，符合預期。展望 4Q26，新興品牌客戶訂單動能強勁，將抵銷布料
淡季造成的營收下滑力道，預期 4Q26 營收將季增，且因產品組合持續優化，及原
材料成本陸續轉嫁，毛利率表現有望穩定回升，推升獲利表現，基此，研究部預估
4Q26 營收 106.2 億元（+0.5％ QoQ，+14.6％ YoY），毛利率 31.5%（+0.32 
pts. QoQ，+1.31 pts. YoY），稅後淨利 18.3 億元（+5.2% QoQ，+15.9％ YoY），
稅後 EPS 6.68 元。 
不利因素消退，產業評價回升，維持買進投資建議：展望後市，儒鴻訂單能見度維
持約六個月，其中，新興品牌客戶訂單動能持續成長強勁，既有品牌客戶亦維持穩
定回補庫存力道。隨著美國期中選舉結果出爐，關稅政策、美伊戰爭此些不利公司
營運及評價的兩大變數皆有望消退，儘管公司維持先前營收及毛利率指引，但隨著
大環境不確定性因素下降、產品組合持續轉佳、原物料成本下跌及向下轉嫁、垂直
整合比重提升，應有利營收及毛利率表現穩步走升，推升獲利表現。公司 2027 年
預計將藉由產能去瓶頸，提升約個位數水準，印尼新廠預期將於 4Q27-1Q28 投

產，規劃月產能為 180 萬件，將分兩期完成。綜合來看，研究部評估儒鴻訂單動
能維持穩健，隨著公司持續導入新產品及新客人加入，有利儒鴻營運表現提升，而

持正向看待儒鴻後市獲利表現。研究部預估 2026 年營收 409 億元（+7.7％YoY），

2027 年營收 442.3 億元（+8.2％YoY），毛利率 31.5%，稅後淨利 76.3 億元（+9.5
％YoY），稅後 EPS 27.79 元。隨著大環境不利因素消退，成衣產業評價應回升，
儒鴻過往本益比區間多位於 12-30 倍，研究部上調儒鴻本益比評價至 18X，依 2027
之 EPS 進行評價，目標價上調至 500 元，投資評等維持買進，依公司去年股利配
發率 74.63%估算，以昨日收盤價來看，目前約具備 4.9%殖利率，應具備下檔殖
利率保護。

## Document 018: 2018年噩梦重演？走势高度相似，纳斯达克反弹之后或还有下一波回调

Source PDF: all report pdf/2018年噩梦重演？走势高度相似，纳斯达克反弹之后或还有下一波回调.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美股跌⼊极端超卖区间，对冲基⾦以近⼗年来最快速度削减仓位，技术性反弹信号逐渐显
现。但美联储仍处于加息周期，资⾦⼊场意愿不⾜。 2018 年四季度，纳指曾在急跌后反
弹，随后再度下探并创出新低。当前市场同样⾯临加息压⼒与⻛险偏好低迷的双重制约。
摘要：
美股抛售潮已将市场推⾄极端超卖状态，但历史经验显⽰，急跌后的反弹未必意味着市场已经
触底，纳斯达克可能仍⾯临新⼀轮回调。
标普 500 指数已跌⼊极度超卖区间，科技股波动率飙升⾄ 2020 年以来最⾼⽔平，对冲基⾦也在以
近⼗年来最快的速度削减仓位。市场恐慌程度与资⾦撤退规模，均已达到罕⻅⽔平。
⾼盛数据显⽰，其恐慌指标（ Vol Panic Index ）上周末收于 9.8 ，满分为 10 。历史上，这⼀⽔平
通常是强烈的逆向加仓信号。但当前美联储仍处于加息周期，情况有所不同：此前恐慌指数超
过 9 且美联储同步加息时，标普 500 指数未来三个⽉平均仅上涨 0.4% 。
更值得警惕的是，当前市场⾛势与 2018 年纳斯达克的⼀轮调整⾼度相似。当年市场在急跌后曾

出现快速反弹，但随后再度下探并创出新低。如今，随着科技股抛售加剧，类似的历史剧本再
次引发市场关注。
科技股恐慌升⾄极值，反弹条件逐步成熟
当前标普 500 指数的超卖程度已处于极端⽔平，同时正在测试⾃ 2022 年底低点以来形成的⻓期趋
势线。
科技股的压⼒更加突出。纳斯达克波动率指数（ VXN ）已升⾄ 2020 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平，超过此
前市场剧烈调整时期的峰值。半导体波动率指数（ VXSMH ）则创下历史新⾼，尽管该指数的数
据仅始于 2022 年。上周五，费城半导体指数（ SOX ）单⽇暴跌及随之⽽来的波动率飙升，更被
市场⼈⼠形容为 “ 载⼊史册 ” 。
从估值来看，部分科技股也已出现明显回调。⾼盛的彼得 · 卡拉汉（ Peter Callahan ）指出，纳
斯达克 100 指数远期市盈率已降⾄ 17 倍，半导体板块更低⾄ 12 ⾄ 13 倍，技术⾯超卖信号明确。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

但估值回落尚未带来明显的抄底需求。卡拉汉指出，当前市场⾛势已经反映企业盈利下调预
期，但实际基本⾯仍相对稳健，盈利预测依然在上修。市场价格与盈利趋势之间的背离，说明
本轮抛售在⼀定程度上受到⻛险偏好下降和仓位调整驱动，⽽⾮企业盈利已经全⾯恶化。
对冲基⾦⼤幅去杠杆，抛售压⼒接近释放尾声？
投资者仓位的变化进⼀步凸显了本轮调整的剧烈程度。
⾼盛主经纪商业务数据显⽰，上周美股单只股票做空规模达到 2022 年 6 ⽉以来最⾼单周⽔平，也
是近⼗年来第三⾼； ETF 空头头⼨单周增加 11% ，创五年来最⼤单周增幅之⼀。上述数据仅统计
⾄上周四，尚未计⼊周五的抛售。
对冲基⾦的净杠杆率也已降⾄极低⽔平。基本⾯多空策略（ L/S ）净杠杆率降⾄ 42.3% ，处于过
去⼀年分布区间的第 1 百分位；经⻉塔调整后的净杠杆率⾃ 10 ⽉初以来累计下降逾 22 个百分点，
⾄ 48.2% ，创五年新低。
⾼盛认为，此轮去杠杆可能已经接近尾声，⽽⾮刚刚开始。不过，市场尚未完全摆脱潜在卖
压： CFTC ⾮商业交易者持仓仍接近⾼位，意味着部分拥挤头⼨仍有进⼀步调整的空间。

与此同时， CTA 策略在纳斯达克 100 期货上的仓位已接近过去⼗年的区间底部。过去六周，系统
性资⾦抛售科技股的速度更创下历史纪录。主动型对冲基⾦与量化资⾦同步减仓，也使科技股
持续承受被动卖盘压⼒。
短伽⻢放⼤双向波动， 2018 年⾛势值得警惕

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
6 条评论
即便去杠杆可能接近尾声，市场也未必会就此企稳。当前⼤量短伽⻢（ Short Gamma ）头⼨意
味着，做市商在市场下跌时可能被迫卖出，上涨时则需要买⼊，从⽽放⼤市场的双向波动。在
波动率已经处于极端⽔平的情况下，这种仓位结构可能令⾏情更加剧烈。
2018 年的历史⾛势，正是当前市场需要警惕的⻛险之⼀。当年纳斯达克经历快速下跌后，曾出
现⼀轮强劲反弹，但反弹未能扭转市场趋势，随后指数再次下探并创出新低。⾼盛及市场观察
⼈⼠⽬前正密切关注这⼀历史⾛势与当下⾏情的相似之处。
这意味着，极端超卖和仓位出清虽然为反弹创造了条件，却不⾜以证明市场已经触底。尤其是
在美联储仍处于加息周期、⻛险偏好尚未恢复的背景下，即使纳斯达克短期反弹，也不排除随
后再次回调。

眼下市场真正需要验证的，不是超卖信号能否触发反弹，⽽是反弹能否获得基本⾯和资⾦⾯的
持续⽀撑。如果抄底资⾦迟迟不⼊场， 2018 年急弹后再创新低的⾛势，仍可能重演。

## Document 026: 2026-11-11_［みずほ経済インサイト］堅調なAI需要で大幅増益が視野_1ea94428

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-11_［みずほ経済インサイト］堅調なAI需要で大幅増益が視野_1ea94428.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

堅調なAI 需要で大幅増益が視野 
投資増につながるかが焦点に

○ 製造業の業績見通しは大幅に改善している。輸出向けを中心とするAI関連財の需要拡大が背景。
2026年度は好業績が期待され、二桁増益が視野に入る 
○ AI関連財の増産を背景に、国内製造業の設備不足感は2025年後半以降強まっている。足元では関
連業種の機械受注が増加しており、AI需要拡大が国内設備投資に波及する兆しがみられる 
○ 設備投資は緩やかな拡大が息長く続く見通し。ただし、米国でデータセンター建設規制が実施さ
れれば、輸出・業績・設備投資の好循環が逆回転に転じるリスクも

１．企業景況感は AI 関連の製造業を中心に改善 
AIの学習・利用に必要なデータセンター投資や半導体需要が世界的に堅調であることを背景に、日
本の製造業の景況感は幅広く改善している。日銀短観（2026年9月調査）では、大企業・製造業の業況
判断DIが6月調査から改善した（図表1）。業種別では、半導体や電子部品などを含む電気機械や、半
導体製造装置を含む生産用機械の改善が際立つ。そのほか、窯業・土石製品、非鉄金属、半導体材料・
素材を扱う化学などでも改善がみられた。ロイター短観（10月調査）でも、「半導体関連市場の需要
が強い」との声が散見され、AI関連需要の堅調さがうかがえる。 
AI関連財の輸出が拡大し、足元で企業の業績見通しは大きく改善している。企業業績の改善を国内
の需要拡大につなげるうえで、次の焦点となるのが設備投資である。企業業績の改善に比べて、投資

### Page 2

2

などの内需への恩恵はまだ明確ではなく、今後設備投資の増加として発現する可能性が期待される。 
２．AI 関連財輸出は好調。2026 年度の製造業は二桁増益が視野に 
世界的なAIサービスの普及に伴い、データセンターや関連機器への投資が拡大している。大規模な
データセンターを保有・運営し、クラウドサービスやAI向けの計算基盤を提供するIT企業、とりわけ
ハイパースケーラーと呼ばれる大規模事業者を中心に、高水準の設備投資を継続する見通しが示され
ていることを踏まえると、AI関連投資の高い伸びは当面持続する見込みである。こうしたなか、モデ
ルの演算を担う処理装置のほか、大量のデータを蓄える記憶装置、サーバー同士をつなぐ通信ケーブ
ル、電力を安定供給する電源装置などといった財において投資需要が高まっている。日本企業が供給
する財についても、電子部品・機器への需要が増加しているほか、AI向け半導体の増産投資は日本の
半導体製造・検査装置や材料への需要を押し上げている。 
図表2は、輸出全体から、WTOの定義に従ってAI関連財を抽出したものだ。AI関連財輸出は2025年終
盤以降加速しており、8月時点では前年比＋29.1％と高い伸びになった。主因は半導体・電子部品で、
旺盛な需要のもとで半導体価格が大幅に上昇し、輸出金額を押し上げている。また、半導体製造・検
査機器や、データセンターに組み込まれる機械・部材、半導体の製造に用いられる原材料・素材など、
幅広いAI関連財が輸出増に寄与している。 
輸出額の高い伸びを受け、製造業では業績見通しが改善している。日銀短観（9月調査）の2026年度
経常利益計画（全規模・製造業）は、6月調査時点から大幅に上方修正され、前年度比＋11.6％の増益
が見込まれる（図表3）。特に電気機械では、上期の堅調な輸出実績を反映して、経常利益計画が大き
く上方修正された。このほか、業況判断DIの改善が目立った業種でも同様の傾向がみられた。堅調な
AI関連需要が下期も続けば、製造業では大幅増益が期待される。

### Page 3

３．AI 関連業種の投資需要は増勢加速の兆しも 
今後の実質GDPへの影響を見通すうえでの焦点は、企業収益の拡大が設備投資を中心とした内需の押
し上げにどの程度繋がるかだ。この点、AI需要の追い風を受け好業績が見込まれることを踏まえれば、
設備投資の伸びはやや物足りなさが否めない。日銀短観（9月調査）によると、製造業の2026年度設備
投資計画は直近5年間では最も低い伸びに留まっている（図表4）。建材や生産設備の価格上昇、金利
上昇に伴う資金調達コストの増加が投資負担となり、好業績のもとでも計画の積み増しを抑えている
可能性がある。 
もっとも、設備投資需要は計画対比で伸び余地がある。AI関連財を中心に増産が進むなか、能力増
強投資の必要性が高まっているためだ。AI関連財の生産は2025年秋以降、はっきりとした増加傾向が
続いている（図表5）。生産・営業用設備判断DI（全規模・製造業）も2025年後半から低下しており、
製造業の設備不足感は強まっている（図表6）。 
実際、AI需要の拡大が今後の設備投資を押し上げる兆しもみられる。図表7は、設備投資の先行指標
とされる機械受注（国内需要向け）を需要者別に分解し、価格変動の影響を取り除いたものだ。これ
をみると2025年夏場以降、情報通信機械、電気機械、はん用・生産用機械といった業種からの受注が
急増していることがわかる。結果として、半導体製造装置をはじめとするAI・データセンター関連品
目では受注残高が積み上がっている（図表8）。 
ただし、機械受注が設備投資として顕在化するまでにはタイムラグを伴うとみられる。そもそも、
受注残高が積み上がっている背景には、旺盛な需要だけでなく、供給面の制約による影響があると考
えられる1。このため、設備投資の増加ペースは緩やかなものにとどまる公算が大きいとみているが、
その分、今後は息の長い投資拡大局面が続くだろう。

図表 5 鉱工業生産 図表6 生産・営業用設備判断DI

（注） AI関連はWTOによるAI-enabling productsの定義に準ずる

### Page 4

4

４．今後は政府の投資支援策も民間投資を後押し。米国の規制強化リスクには要注意 
以上のように、先行きの設備投資は、設備不足感の強まるAI関連産業の投資需要に支えられ、緩や
かに増勢を強めると見込まれる。 
また今後は、政府の政策支援が、投資コストの負担軽減を通じて、設備投資を後押しするとみられ
る。7月末に始動した政府の「大胆な投資促進税制」では、一定の要件を満たす国内投資について、即
時償却や税額控除の適用を認める減税措置が設けられた。さらに、2027年度予算の編成では、特別予
算枠として『「強く豊かな日本」投資枠』を設け、民間投資を促す支援策が検討されている。具体的
な支援策が整えば、複数年度にわたる投資計画の実行を支えよう。 
なお、11月3日に投開票された米国中間選挙の結果を受け、米国におけるデータセンター投資が下振
れるリスクにも触れておきたい。同選挙では、データセンター建設計画の多いテキサス州やジョージ
ア州の州知事選などにおいて、建設の一時停止を掲げる民主党候補が勝利した。米国では、データセ
ンター建設規制に関して州政府や地方自治体が大きな権限を持つ。 
実際、市場ではAI関連企業の業績・投資下振れが意識され、グローバルにハイテク銘柄の株価が急
落した。仮に今後、米国でデータセンター建設が規制されれば、日本でも半導体・電子部品や製造装
置などの輸出が減少し、企業業績が下押しされる可能性がある。その際、AI関連財が減産に転じて設
備不足感が弱まれば、日本国内における能力増強投資の需要も後退しかねない。新知事の任期開始お
よび新議会の招集は2027年1月だが、当面の米国政治動静については注意深くみていく必要があろう。

1 なお、供給制約に起因して、資本財の輸入浸透度（国内供給に占める輸入品の割合）が一層上昇する可能性には留意が必要で
ある。今後、国内の供給余力が限られるなかで投資需要が一層輸入品に向かえば、国産資本財への需要が減退し、投資需要増に
よる GDP の押し上げ効果が限られるリスクがある。

## Document 062: 2026年10月财政数据点评：财政支出增速持续改善

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月财政数据点评：财政支出增速持续改善.pdf
Report source: 开源证券
Date: 2026-11-24
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

事件点评 
请务必参阅正文后面的信息披露和法律声明 1 / 7

2026年 11月 23日

《合计债券托管量环比多增，商业银
行杠杆率提升—2026年 10月债市托
管数据点评》-2026.11.20 
《季节性因素与假期错位扰动下经济
数据仍需观察—2026年 10月经济数
据点评》-2026.11.18 
《政府债支撑社融增速，企业一般贷
款有所修复—2026年 10月金融数据
点评》-2026.11.17  
财政支出增速持续改善 
——2026年 10月财政数据点评

陈曦（分析师） 王帅中（联系人）  
chenxi2@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790521100002 
wangshuaizhong@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790125070016

事件：财政部公布 2026年 10月财政数据，10月当月全国一般公共预算收入同
比提升 3.4%（前值-0.1%，下同），支出同比提升 0.46%（+0.03%）； 10月全国政
府性基金预算收入同比提升 9.5%（-15.9%），支出同比提升 17.2%（-12.3%）。  
⚫ 10月财政数据的关注点 
10月税收收入同比增长 5.9%（+0.2%），较前值提升 5.7pct，较前值回升幅度较
大，其中个人所得税大幅提升拉动税收收入同比回升，个人所得税同比提升
89.5%，较前值提升 79.0pct，主要系离岸信托纳税人就离岸信托收益申报缴纳
个人所得税，10月当月入库超 1200亿元，带动了全国 10月个人所得税收入增
长近 90%。国内消费税同比增长由负转正，同比增长 4.1%，较前值提升 12.6pct，
2026 年以来进口环节增值税和消费税始终保持高速增长，与进出口数据保持一
致。国内增值税同比转负，较前值下降 7.4pct，与 10月生产端经济数据表现基
本一致，10月工业增加值同比增速放缓。10月证券交易印花税同比下降 33.7%，
较前值下降 22.2pct，连续两个月同比增速为负，9月以来权益市场的回调与 2025
年同期的高基数或为主要原因。非税收入同比下降 23.6%（-1.0%），同比增速较
前值大幅下降 22.5pct。 
土地出让收入下滑持续拖累政府基金性收入。10 月政府性基金收入同比提升
9.5%，其中土地出让收入同比下降 9.0%，土地出让收入下滑拖累政府基金性收
入，10月土地出让累计收入占政府性基金累计收入比重为 63.6%，占比有所下降，
尽管近期一线城市商品住宅销售价格环比有所上涨，但土地财政退潮或仍将持续
拖累政府基金性收入。 
截至 10 月底，全国一般公共预算支出 22.80万亿元，支出规模为年初预算的
75.99%；全国一般公共预算收入 19.57万亿元，收入规模为年初预算的 88.66%。 
⚫ 一般公共预算：10月收入同比增速转正、支出同比维持于正区间 
收入：10月，一般公共预算收入同比提升 3.4%（-0.1%），其中中央收入同比增
长 2.9%（+0.2%），地方收入同比增长 3.9%（-0.3%）。税收收入中，国内增值
税、关税、土地增值税、耕地占用税、环境保护税和证券交易印花税当月同比较
2026年 9月有所下降。非税收入同比下降 23.6%（-1.0%），较前值下降 22.5pct。 
支出：10月，一般公共预算支出同比提升 0.46%（+0.03%）， 维持于正区间，
其中中央支出同比增长 1.9%（+4.6%），地方支出同比提升 0.1%（-0.8%）， 央
地之间支出分化小幅收窄，财政支出同比较 9月有所回升。 
⚫ 政府性基金预算：10月收入、支出同比均回升至正区间 
收入：10月，政府性基金收入同比提升 9.5%（-15.9%），中央收入同比提升 155.8%
（+13.7%），地方收入同比下降 5.2%（-18.4%），其中土地出让收入同比减少 9.0%
（-23.2%）。支出：10月，政府性基金支出同比提升 17.2%（-12.3%），中央支出
同比增长 13.3 %（+7.5%），地方支出同比提升 17.5%（-13.1%），其中土地出让
支出同比下降 0.6%（-18.7%）。 
⚫ 债市观点：债券收益率或将上行，警惕债市的反转风险 
对于债市，我们认为债券收益率或将上行，警惕债市的反转风险。 
第一，市场大多观点认为，后续 DR001将围绕 1.25%运行，这存在落空的可能
性，或有利多出尽的风险。 
第二，金融时报连发两文指出“央行研究切换公开市场操作主力品种，属于货币
政策框架改革的一项举措，不同于通常讲的货币政策降息加息…市场各方要避免
将央行操作机制改革举措当作降息来炒作”，或指明监管对当前债市的态度趋于
谨慎。 
第三，拥挤度层面，当前公募等非银机构债券久期已达近年高位，特别是经过这
轮换锚发酵后，拥挤度再次提升，当前存在拥挤度调整、估值回调风险。 
第四，基本面层面，国内经济有触底回升迹象，政府债发行速度加快，政策逐渐
落地下经济动能边际修复，当前债市的定价尚未反应基本面的边际修复，这也是
潜在的风险。 
⚫ 风险提示：政策变化超预期；经济变化超预期。

特别声明
《证券期货投资者适当性管理办法》、《证券经营机构投资者适当性管理实施指引（试行）》已于2017年7月1日起正
式实施。根据上述规定，开源证券评定此研报的风险等级为R3（中风险），因此通过公共平台推送的研报其适用的
投资者类别仅限定为专业投资者及风险承受能力为C3、C4、C5的普通投资者。若您并非专业投资者及风险承受能
力为C3、C4、C5的普通投资者，请取消阅读，请勿收藏、接收或使用本研报中的任何信息。
因此受限于访问权限的设置，若给您造成不便，烦请见谅！感谢您给予的理解与配合。
分析师承诺
本研究报告的署名人员具有中国证券业协会授予的证券投资咨询执业资格或相当的专业胜任能力，以勤勉的职业态
度，独立、客观地出具本报告，并对内容和观点负责。本报告清晰准确地反映了署名人员的研究观点，所包含的分
析基于各种假设，不同假设可能导致分析结果出现重大不同。本报告采用的各种估值方法及模型均有其局限性，估
值结果不保证所涉及证券能够在该价格交易。本报告署名人员保证他们报酬的任何一部分不曾与，不与，也将不会

与本报告中具体的推荐意见或观点有直接或间接的联系。
股票投资评级说明
评级 说明
证券评级
买入（Buy） 预计相对强于市场表现 20%以上；
增持（outperform） 预计相对强于市场表现 5%～20%；
中性（Neutral） 预计相对市场表现在－5%～＋5%之间波动；
减持（underperform） 预计相对弱于市场表现 5%以下。
行业评级
看好（overweight） 预计行业超越整体市场表现；
中性（Neutral） 预计行业与整体市场表现基本持平；
看淡（underperform） 预计行业弱于整体市场表现。
备注：评级标准为以报告日后的 6~12个月内，证券相对于市场基准指数的涨跌幅表现，其中 A股基准指数为沪
深 300指数、港股基准指数为恒生指数、新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针
对做市转让标的）、美股基准指数为标普 500或纳斯达克综合指数。我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同
的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议；投资者买入或者卖出证券的决

定取决于个人的实际情况，比如当前的持仓结构以及其他需要考虑的因素。投资者应阅读整篇报告，以获取比较
完整的观点与信息，不应仅仅依靠投资评级来推断结论。
分析、估值方法的局限性说明
本报告所包含的分析基于各种假设，不同假设可能导致分析结果出现重大不同。本报告采用的各种估值方法及模型
均有其局限性，估值结果不保证所涉及证券能够在该价格交易。

### Page 1

M
 Update
Silergy Corp.  | Asia Pacific
  3Q26 Margin miss from higher 
production cost and opex; stay 
UW 
3Q26 results miss from incremental production cost and higher opex: Silergy’s 
3Q26 GM of 47.4% and OPM of 13.3% missed MSe and consensus despite a better 
product mix (faster growth in AI and automotive). We attribute the miss to rising 
foundry/OSAT costs and higher AI datacenter R&D. Management guided opex to 
stay roughly flat in 1H27 and that it could be higher in 2H27 with new hires.
AI-related revenue and Gen4 process are key drivers for 2027 margin recovery: 
Management said 2027 revenue could grow at least 20-30% YoY, with AI-related 
revenue at c.25% and Gen4 at 50% of total revenue by end-2027. Management also 
guided 2027 GM to be >50% while continuing passing on cost to customers.
Automotive revenue could account for 20% of 2027 total revenue: Management 
expects strong automotive growth in 2027, with dollar content per high-end vehicle 
rising from US$220 in 2026 to US$270-300 in 2027.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Silergy Corp. (6415.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 9.5% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6%
risk premium, beta of 1.25), an intermediate growth rate of 13.5%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Gross margin expansion amid stabilized competition
n A surge in LED demand as pricing drops to the sweet spot
n Greater-than-expected benefits from China's support in local semiconductors

n Higher-than-expected dividend payout ratio
Risks to Downside
n Lower gross margin amid intensified competition
n Weaker-than-expected benefits from China's support in local semiconductors
n Lower-than-expected dividend payout ratio

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 154: 261117_ubs_aspeed-DG

Source PDF: all report pdf/261117_ubs_aspeed-DG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 18
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 17 November 2026
Global Research
ASPEED Technology Inc
Near-term BMC momentum peaking;
downgrade to Neutral
Three reasons for our downgrade to Neutral
We remain constructive about Aspeed’s growth potential and competitive positioning
longer term, but are downgrading the stock from Buy to Neutral on limited upside vs
expectation in 2027-28. Key considerations: 1) Aspeed’s BMC unit growth is highly tied
to overall Cloud AI TAM expansion, which is at rising risk especially for 2028 after the US

mid-terms and constraint of power / datacenter construction; 2) our analysis shows that
Aspeed may have over-shipped vs end market server units in 2026, leading to
uncertainty of order normalization in 2027 if tight supply eases / demand slows; 3) we
see limited shipment upside in next several quarters from high base in Q426.
Possible order moderation in 2027, after some over-build this year
Aspeed's BMC units grew 78% YoY to reach ~34mn in 2026, of which we estimate

9.5mn units for accelerator servers, and 23.5mn units for general purpose servers. Our
analysis indicates that Aspeed's BMC shipment for general purpose server likely exceeds
the end market server units at ~22.7m this year, implying excess inventory build at
hyperscalers which could cap the upside into 2027. We note that healthy shipment ratio
of Aspeed's BMC units vs end market should be ~80-90% considering Aspeed's 80%
market share, with 5-10% upside for switch, power supply etc. The ratio likely rises to

104% in 2026 from 70%+ in 2024-25. See Figure 1-3 for our analysis.   We lower 2027
BMC units from 48.2mn to 42.4mn, assuming the BMC shipment to reach near term
peak of 11-12m/Q in Q426-Q127, and to soften moderately to ~10m/Q in H227. We
still consider robust growth of AI accelerater BMCs at 82% in 2027E vs 76% in 2026E.
Product roadmap reinforces long-term leadership
Longer term, we remain constructive on Aspeed’s strong execution, accelerated product

roadmap, and rising silicon content per server. Aspeed remains highly focused on BMC
development, unlike  many competitors where BMC is one of several priorities. Its
roadmap, including more advanced BMCs such as the 6nm AST2800, enhanced security
features, and adjacent products such as SMC, I/O expanders and FPGA integration,
should reinforce its leading position in the Cloud AI market over the next 3-5 years. See
our view on Marvell’s infrastructure management controller (IMC) introduction.

Valuation: D/G to Neutral from Buy; lower target to NT$10,000 from NT$19,000
We trim 2027/28E EPS by 14%/11%. Our new estimates are 10/12% below VA
consensus. Aspeed's order outlook remains robust for 2027, but we see risk of
moderation in coming quarters. Downgrade to Neutral from Buy. Our new target of NT
$10,000 is based on 30x 2027E PE, as we roll back from 43x 2027-28E PE given the
rising uncertainty. 30x PE is below Aspeed's historical mid-cycle of 36x, but we view

reasonable considering the potential demand slowdown and 30% LT earnings CAGR.

### Page 2

ASPEED Technology Inc UBS Research
Thesis MapUBS Research THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can BMC units continue to grow in the next two years after rising 78% in 2026E?
Although we remain constructive about Aspeed's long term execution and technology leadership,
we see a possible order moderation into 2027 after over-build in 2026E. We estimate that BMC
shipment will rise 78% YoY in 2026E and reach around 34m total BMC shipment units with 23.5m

dedicated for general purpose servers—this suggests potential pre-building of BMC inventory by
hyperscalers, as end market general purpose server units are only at 22.7m units in 2026E. In
addition, we are also monitoring the risk of AI data center buildout slowdown in 2028 post the recent
US elections and power constraints. We now forecast BMC shipment growth to moderate in
2027E/28E, with 25%/13% YoY unit shipment growth respectively.
Q: Can Aspeed maintain its dominant 70%-plus market share in server BMCs?

Yes. We expect Aspeed to maintain its market share in the next 3-4 years due to 1) We expect
migration to AST2700 to accelerate in 2027 from a mild 2026 ramp up, with new Intel and AMD CPU
platforms as well as key cloud ASIC projects and Nvidia's new Rubin Ultra AI GPU platform in 2027; 2)
Aspeed has enhanced its BMC offering by embedding flexibility to satisfy customer requirements; 3)
Aspeed is expanding its product portfolio to I/O expanders that integrate functionalities built on

FPGA, RoT security chips, and mini-BMCs (SMCs). 4) Aggressive business strategy to build inventory
for hyperscalers since 2025, which we believe should limit opportunity for competitors.
Q: Can Aspeed maintain overall gross margins at a high-60s level?
Yes. We expect Aspeed's GM to remain in the high-60s over the next few years. Drivers include: 1) the
roll-out of the higher-end AST2700 series, which could solidify Aspeed's competitive position and

help it sustain market share; 2) Aspeed managing back-end logistics for AST2700, reducing reliance
on GUC; and 3) Aspeed raising prices (effective Dec 1) for BMCs to pass through cost inflation.
We are constructive for Aspeed's long-term competitive positioning but are downgrading it to
Neutral. Our key considerations: 1) Aspeed’s BMC unit growth is highly tied to overall Cloud AI TAM
expansion, which is at rising risk especially for 2028 after the US mid-terms and constraint of power /

datacenter construction; 2) our analysis shows that Aspeed may have over-shipped vs end market
server units in 2026, leading to uncertainty of order normalization in 2027 if tight supply eases /
demand slows; 3) we see limited shipment upside in next several quarters from high base in Q426.
1) Our analysis indicates that Aspeed's BMC shipment for general purpose server likely exceeds the
end market server units at ~22.7m this year, implying excess inventory build at hyperscalers which

could cap the upside into 2027. 2) The outcome of recent US mid-terms creates higher uncertainty
for new AI data center build-out in 2028.
Aspeed's share price has rallied ~65% YTD (vs Taipei Exchange Index's 11% gain), but declined 54%
from its peak in Sep. The stock is now trading at 29x/22x 2027/28E PE, de-rated from its peak of
around 63x/48x in Sept, suggesting the market is taking a more conservative stance regarding overall
Cloud AI outlook and Aspeed's growth.

Company Description
ASPEED is the world's largest baseboard management controller (BMC) chipset provider.
ASPEED introduced the first generation of server management processors in 2005. It acquired
Emulex Pilot's remote server management chip business in 2016. Besides server management

chipsets, ASPEED also produces PC/AV extension solutions and image processing system-on-
chips (SoCs). In 2023, server management chipsets accounted for 88% of total sales.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We derive our price target for Aspeed from PE valuation methodology relative to its historical
trading range.
We believe key upside risks for Aspeed include: 1) stronger-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers and from AI servers with less crowding out of traditional

servers; 2) further market share expansion; 3) faster-than-expected server platform upgrades;
4) faster-than-expected progress in expanding the addressable content within servers.
We believe key downside risks for Aspeed include: 1) weaker-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers; 2) market share losses or more intense industry competition; 3)
slower-than-expected server platform upgrades; 4) slower-than-expected progress in
expanding the addressable content within servers.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

股價相對大盤走勢

世芯-KY(3661 TT) Buy

新客戶加入，挹注 2027 年營運成長，建議 Buy。  
投資建議：世芯-KY 受惠北美 CSP 客戶 N3 AI 加速器量產，
2026 年營運大幅成長；展望 2027 年，除既有 N3 專案持續放量
外，美系新客戶約 10 億美元專案預計自 2Q27 起貢獻量產營收，
將推動營收及獲利進一步成長。中長期 N2 AI 加速器亦持續推
進，預計 2028 年進入量產，成為下一階段成長動能，預估 2026
年及 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 113.66 元及 193.02 元。惟隨量產
(COT)業務占比提高，營收成長未必同步帶動毛利率提升，且
CSP 持續提升自主設計能力，將是影響世芯-KY 長期商業模式
及評價的重要風險。我們認為上述風險主要影響中長期估值上
限，但未來 12 個月仍受惠 N3 放量及新客戶加入，基本面成長
趨勢明確。因此建議 Buy，目標價 4,635 元 (PER24X)。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元：北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片已
正式啟動量產出貨，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億
元，QoQ+240.99%。晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營
收產生約 1 億美元之負面影響。受量產業務營收佔比拉升影響，
3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個百
分點。3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
取得美系新客戶訂單: 世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽
訂重大合約並展開執行，該案量約 10 億美元，合約已正式生效
並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季增加，該專案確
定應用於 AI 領域。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對
象，非中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出
規模，研判最具可能性之潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之
北美雲端巨頭；世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切
入合作，有效緩解客戶端工程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指
出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全新商業模式之開端，
該新客戶將躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。

### Page 2

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

2 群益投顧 
  世芯-KY ESG：  
世芯電子身為全球 IC 設計服務領導廠商，為確保所有產品皆為符合環境安
全衛生規定之綠色產品，對供應鏈管理不遺餘力，與供應商合作將綠色環保
的政策及概念推廣到整個供應，並一起朝向減少地球環境之衝擊共同努力，
其中主要包括： 
有害物質管制 
符合世芯電子相關規範及國內、外法令等國際環境規範 
滿足世芯電子新外包商評鑑程序 
滿足世芯電子外包商管理程序 
滿足世芯電子環境管理物質管理程序 
歐盟危害物質限用指令 (EU RoHS Compliance) 
歐盟新化學品政策 (REACH) 
無鹵材料 (Halogen Free) 
綠色物料之承認管理 
供應商提出第三方對產品生產材料所作的環境物質分析報告或證明文件。 
綠色資訊平台之建置 
建構系統化之 IT 資料庫輔助管理系統，藉此平台，便利上下游廠商綠色零
件之交換，並帶動上游廠商綠色零件供應的能力，降低環保要求的衝擊程
度。 
建立環境管理系統 - 供應商必須擁有 ISO14001 環境管理系統或其他相關
環境管理系統相關驗證。 
無衝突礦產保證書-(Declaration of Metal Conflict-Free) 
衝突金屬係指來自剛果民主共和國衝突礦區之礦物，類別有鈮鉭鐵礦，錫
石，黑鎢礦與黃金等。這些礦物提煉成鉭(Ta)、錫(Sn)、鎢(W) 、鈷(Co) 和
金 (Au) 等，分別用於電子和其他產品。世芯電子強烈要求供應商提供的原
物料中不得間接或直接使用衝突礦產。 
企業社會責任 (CSR) - 確保供應商落實企業社會責任。 
業務連續性計劃 (BCP) 
供應商須有一套基於業務運行規律的流程，使組織在突發事件面前能夠迅
速作出反應，以確保關鍵業務功能可以持續，而不造成業務中斷或業務流程
本質的改變。 
綠色管理稽核 - 世芯-KY 每年持續對供應商進行稽核、改善及教育訓練，
經由系統化運作與管理，確保提供品質精良之卓越產品給客戶與消費者。

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

3 群益投顧 
N3 貢獻增加，3Q26 營收 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%，稅後淨利 32.51 億
元，QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元： 
北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片 已於 05/2026 正式啟動量產出貨，之後營
收逐月向上，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%。
在產能限制方面，晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營收產生約 1 億
美元之負面影響，若無此類供應限制，單季營收原可增加 1 億美元。資料中
心以外之其他應用在 3Q26 合計創造超過 1 億美元營收，其中中國車用 ADAS
需求維持強勁，客戶次世代晶片如期於 4Q26 進行流片(Tape-out)。  
3Q26 各應用佔公司營收比重，HPC 佔公司營收比重 87%，佔比仍為最高，
相較 2Q26 的 50%與 1Q26 的 70%大幅提升。消費性電子(Consumer)佔比降
至 8%，低於 2Q26 的 16%與 1Q26 的 17%。 Networking 佔 5%，較 2Q26 的
32%與 1Q26 的 10%下降。利基型(Niche，含超級電腦、虛擬貨幣、柏青哥
機台、醫療設備與監視系統)佔 0%，低於 2Q26 的 1%與 1Q26 的 3%。  
製程營收比重，N3/N2 佔公司營收比重大幅躍升至 80%，較 2Q26 的 47%與
1Q26 的 21%大幅增加，主因北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器持續放量出貨。
7nm/5nm 佔比降至 15%，較 2Q26 的 40%下滑。16/12nm 佔比為 3%，較 2Q26
的 6%下降。28nm 及以下製程佔比為 1%，低於 2Q26 的 7%。  
地區營收分布隨著 N3 加速器放量出貨，地區分布再度出現結構性轉變：北
美地區佔比穩居第一大市場，達 84%(2Q26 為 51%， 1Q26 為 23%)。亞太地
區佔比降至 12%(2Q26 為 25%， 1Q26 為 47%)，車用晶片出貨維持穩健。其
他地區佔 4%(2Q26 為 17%， 1Q26 為 22%)。日本地區佔 1%(2Q26 為 7%，
1Q26 為 8%)。   
毛利率方面，3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個
百分點，為產品組合的最差情境，主因量產營收比重大幅拉升，致使委託設
計 (NRE)營收貢獻比例低於預期，加上數個專案之 NRE 認列產生遞延所致。
不過營業利益與稅後淨利同步創高。3Q26 世芯-KY 稅後淨利 32.51 億元，
QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
N2 專案將在 2026 年底 Tape-Out：  
世芯-KY 承接之美系 CSP 次世代 N2 AI 加速器專案，將採用異質小晶片
(Heterogeneous Chiplet) 架構。晶片總面積維持在約 4 個光罩尺寸，但相較
於 N3 世代，N2 專案之運算晶粒(Compute Die) 配置、內部互連架構
(Interconnect)及封裝型式(2.5D/3D)均出現顯著改動。由於小晶片設計涉及
多個獨立晶( 包括主運算晶粒與 I/O 晶粒) ，各晶粒之開發週轉時間
(Turnaround Time)不同，專案將採取分階段進行多次定案(Tape-out)之模式。
2026 年底 Tape-out ， 2027 年底放量 
次世代 N2 加速器已進入最終實作階段，預計自 4Q26 開始展開流片，目標
於 4Q27 達成小量試產(Low volume production) ，並於 2028 年迎來量產放
量，推動世芯-KY 自 2028 年初邁入全新成長階段。N2 與 N3 在晶片設計複
雜度上有顯著差異，N3 主要為 SoC 設計結合 2.5D 封裝，而 N2 雖維持 2.5D
封裝架構，但已全面轉向 Chiplet 架構搭配全環繞閘極(GAA)製程技術。世
芯 -KY 負責 N2 晶片各個部分之實體設計並進行整合，其在 N2 專案之設計
參與深度與整體專案規模均顯著大於 N3。專案不依賴特定同業之第三方 IP，

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

4 群益投顧 
均採用通用型解決方案。在商業與生產模式上，世芯-KY 為 N2 之唯一實體
設計服務商，而在量產階段，客戶目前承諾僅會有客戶本身與世芯-KY 兩方
直接向台積電下單投片，世芯-KY 在 N2 之供應鏈地位與競爭優勢將較 N3
更為穩固。關於小晶片整合，包含 I/O Die 在內之晶片均由世芯-KY 統籌實
體設計，未來若因應 UALink 或 NVLink Fusion 等技術規格變更推出其他版
本之 I/O Die，世芯-KY 亦具備承接物理設計之先機。 
COT 合作模式短中期是有利世芯-KY，但長期要觀察 CSP 廠的全面自主設
計的進度： 
各大 CSP 為降低對傳統純 ASIC 廠商(如 Broadcom)高定價的依賴並實現
Cost-down，正全面轉向 COT 模式(客戶負責前端設計，世芯負責實體設計
與後端整合)。專案朝向「運算晶粒(Compute die)與  I/O 晶粒分工設計」發
展，世芯已與潛在客戶洽談負責運算晶粒並與外部 I/O 晶片整合的模式。
CSP 客戶轉向 COT 模式，本質上源於 3 項戰略考量： 
避免供應商鎖定(Vendor Lock-in) ：多數競爭對手採用垂直整合的封閉模式
(Captive Model)，從 IP、設計服務到封裝一條龍自主掌控，客戶一旦導入即
難以切換。CSP 客戶對此高度警惕，傾向採用第三方開放解決方案以保有供
應商多元化的彈性，降低單一供應商造成的議價劣勢與供應鏈風險。 
成本透明度與預算自主性：COT 模式下，客戶對晶圓、IP、設計服務及其他
供應鏈成本具有較高的掌握度，實際報酬及成本分攤方式則依個別專案合
約而定。相較於封閉模式下由供應商主導整體產品定價，COT 模式有助於
客戶提高成本控管能力與預算可預測性。 
規模擴張下的成本節省潛力： AI 晶片專案的出貨量與單位價值均遠高於傳
統晶片，隨著量產規模持續放大，COT 模式下透明且固定的利潤結構所帶
來的成本節省效益將等比例放大，對 CSP 客戶的財務影響不容忽視。 
然而，COT 模式對世芯-KY 的財務表現構成若干限制： 
COT 模式下的獲利空間取決於合約條件與服務範圍：COT 模式下，客戶通
常對專案成本及供應鏈有較高的掌握度，世芯-KY 的獲利則取決於其承接
的設計服務範圍、專案管理責任及個別合約的報酬安排。相較於自行掌握完
整產品定價與供應鏈的模式，若專案採成本加成或事先約定服務報酬的方
式，世芯-KY 透過晶片價格上漲或供應鏈成本變動擴大利潤的空間可能較
為有限。因此，未來毛利率及獲利表現，仍須觀察各專案的服務內容、合約
條件及營收組合變化。 
多方協作帶來的執行複雜度與供應鏈風險： COT 模式涉及晶圓廠、IP 供應
商、基板廠、封裝廠及測試廠等多個外部夥伴的協同整合，任一環節的產能
短缺或交期延誤均可能波及整體專案進度。世芯-KY 在 N7 世代曾因 ABF 基
板短缺而承受供應鏈壓力，此一歷史案例揭示開放生態系模式下供應鏈管
理的內在脆弱性。儘管公司已大幅增加供應商來源以分散風險，但多源管理
本身亦帶來額外的協調成本與品質控管挑戰。 
IP 自主能力缺失，技術話語權受限：世芯-KY 目前選擇與 Synopsys 等第三
方 IP 供應商合作，而非自行開發 IP。此策略雖降低研發資本支出，卻也意
味著公司在核心技術環節缺乏自主掌控能力，在特定 IP 出現供應限制或授
權條件變動時，可能面臨被動局面，長期而言亦限制公司在技術差異化上的
發展空間。

### Page 5

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

5 群益投顧 
規模成長不必然帶動利潤率同步提升：在固定利潤百分比的框架下，營收規
模的擴大主要反映的是晶圓與供應鏈成本的等比例放大，而非公司自身附
加價值的提升。換言之，世芯-KY 的獲利成長高度依賴出貨量的持續擴張，
而非利潤率的結構性改善，此一特性使公司的盈利模式更接近於量能驅動
型 (Volume-driven)，而非價值驅動型(Value-driven)。  
整體而言，世芯-KY 等 IC 設計服務公司的商業模式恰好能針對不同客戶的
能力缺口提供精準介入。無論客戶需求是後端實體設計落地、部分實體設計
協作、I/O Chiplet 的 IP 整合，乃至純量產委外，世芯均具備對應的服務能
量。此種高度彈性的模組化接案模式，使公司得以滲透不同成熟度的客戶群
體，有效降低拿單門檻。 
總而言之，CSP 晶片自研能力的持續演進是評估世芯-KY 長期投資價值不
可迴避的核心風險。從演進路徑來看，CSP 通常由依賴完整 ASIC 服務起步，
逐步過渡至主導架構設計但委外實體落地，最終邁向全面設計自主。世芯-
KY 目前所享有的設計服務需求，本質上是 CSP 處於此過渡階段所產生的結
構性紅利，一旦 CSP 設計能力全面成熟，世芯-KY 的角色將面臨從「策略
性設計夥伴」退化為「可替代供應鏈中介」的根本性風險，商業模式的可持
續性將受到挑戰。短期而言，N3 乃至 N2 先進製程實體設計的高度複雜性，
以及多代合作累積的工程默契，仍構成一定的切換成本緩衝。然而隨著 EDA
工具智能化持續壓低設計門檻，此一護城河的侵蝕速度是要警惕。群益認
為，世芯-KY 能否在當前紅利窗口期內成功深化 IP 能力、拓展先進封裝技
術佈局並實現客戶多元化，將是決定其長期商業模式能否升級躍遷的關鍵
觀察指標。 
N3 專案為 2026 年和 2027 年主要的營收貢獻： 
世芯-KY 目前有超過 60 項 FinFET 專案和 20 個以上 CoWoS 案子，平均每
年 20~30 new tape-out，且獲得多個 N3 專案，也有 N2 專案進行中。其中最
重要的專案是為北美 CSP 開發的 N3 AI 加速器(Trainium 3)。該專案在 4Q25
啟動，2Q26 末已進入量產。N5 完整的專案營收貢獻約 35 億美元，N3 的營
收貢獻會高很多，除主要客戶專案之外，世芯-KY 還贏得來自北美客戶的多
個 N3 專案，包括網路客戶以及北美和歐洲的主要網路設備廠商。 
現行 N3 晶片已符合各項測試標準且良率維持在合理水準，目前正全速出
貨，為 2026 年最大營收貢獻專案，亦將維持為 2027 年最大營收來源。管理
層預期當前世代專案將量產約 2 年時間，隨後由次世代接替。該專案預期於
4Q26 營收呈現小幅季增，並於 2027 年呈現大幅增長。世芯-KY 過去因營運
資金有限而在該專案初期未深化溝通，但後續成功自競爭對手處贏回此專
案，目前主要營運與產能限制已獲得解決，已做好承接更大出貨規模之準
備。 
取得美系新客戶訂單，預期於 2027 年躍升為世芯-KY 第二大客戶： 
世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽訂重大合約並展開執行，該專案整
體生命週期累計營收規模約 10 億美元，量產營收預計自 2Q27 開始貢獻，
後續逐季增加。已正式生效並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季
維持季增趨勢，該專案確定應用於 AI 領域，推估採用台積電 N3 先進製程，
專案資產由雙方共同擁有。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對象，非
中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出規模，市場推測可能

6 群益投顧 
為潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之北美雲端巨頭 Meta 或 Microsoft；
世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切入合作，有效緩解客戶端工
程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全
新商業模式之開端，具備良好獲利能力且長期可持續，該新客戶自 2Q27 起
之量產放量將推動其躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。 
2027 年 N3 案子貢獻持續，及美系新客戶加入，預估 2026 年和 2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 113.66 元和 193.02 元： 
世芯-KY 2026 年 ~2028 年營收將會再大幅成長，因為 1)北美大客戶新世代
產品 N3 量產和下一代設計合作，公司預計其關鍵客戶在 2028 年前將有強
勁且可持續的營收貢獻；營收從 2Q26 開始顯著躍升。2)美系大型巨頭新客
戶的 10 億美元，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季季增。3)車用業務
ADAS 方面，預計產品將成為從 2026 年開始的主要營收貢獻者之一。下一
代產品的開發已在進行中，確保營收連續性並為業務帶來強勁動能，直到
2028 年。 
受惠於 N3 AI加速器量產出貨小幅季增，疊加次世代 N2 專案展開流片(Tape-
out)與大型設計案啟動，4Q26 營收預估將達到 8.5~9 億美元區間，改寫單季
歷史新高。由於量產營收比重仍高達 80%以上，4Q26 毛利率預估將維持在
18~19.5%區間，公司預期 2026 年全年毛利率將達 20%以上。預估 2026 年
稅後 EPS 113.66 元。 
邁入 2027 年，1Q27 營收預估小幅拉回，自 2Q27 起，隨著規模達 10 億美
元的美國新大客戶量產營收正式啟動，營運將重啟逐季強勁增長動能，單季
營收將突破 10 億美元大關。3Q27 至 4Q27 在現有 N3 專案擴大出貨以及次
世代 N2 加速器於 4Q27 展開小量產(Low volume production) 挹注下，預估
2027 年稅後 EPS 193.02 元。 
2028 年次世代 N2 加速器進入量產： 
展望 2028 年，世芯-KY 大客戶的次世代 N2 加速器進入大規模量產階段，
由於 N2 專案轉向全環繞閘極(GAA)與 Chiplet 先進架構，世芯-KY 表示將
負責全晶片實體設計與整合，晶片價值量與專案規模將顯著超越 N3 世代。
因此管理層評估 2028 年營收具備再次翻倍之潛力，但群益先以保守情境預
估。 
在營運擴張過程中，世芯-KY 面臨兩大核心營運限制，分別為營運資金
(Working Capital)承載力以及高階研發人力瓶頸。在資金層面，管理層明確
指出 2028 年營收成長的瓶頸已非市場需求，而是資產負債表的承載能力。
由於雲端服務商(CSP)先進製程晶圓與先進封裝代工代採購金額極為龐大，
支撐 40 億美元以上營收需要巨額營運資金墊付晶圓廠貨款，公司資產負債
表結構將直接決定能承接的量產規模上限。在人力層面，單一先進製程大型
專案往往需要高達 150 位工程師投入，在 N3 出貨、N2 實作與新客戶專案
同步推進下，研發資源短缺成為主要限制，迫使公司採取高度篩選專案之策
略，僅承接具備實質商業營收之大型案件。為突破研發人力天花板，世芯-
KY 目前正積極對外擴編，預計 2026 年底員工人數將突破千人規模，並在
前端實作與驗證導入專屬內部 AI 訓練模型以大幅節省中段研發人力，但在
最終實體設計階段仍高度仰賴頂尖工程人力，高階人才擴充速度將是公司
長期承接新專案的關鍵決定因素。

10 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아쉬웠습니다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추정치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

## Document 278: AI服务器临交付暴涨45%，台积电收紧账期要现款：黄仁勋刚走，全球供应链撕破脸

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI服务器临交付暴涨45%，台积电收紧账期要现款：黄仁勋刚走，全球供应链撕破脸.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI 产业链正从 “ 互相托底 ” 转向 “ 现款现货 ” ：戴尔等服务器⼚商临近交付⼤幅加价，台积电收
紧信⽤额度、要求超额订单现⾦⽀付，英伟达也开始收紧融资⽀持。随着上下游不再愿意
垫资兜底，⼿握真⾦⽩银、拥有真实订单的玩家才能继续扩张，⾼度依赖融资和供应商垫
资的项⽬将⾯临更⼤压⼒。
摘要：
过去⼏年，⻩仁勋不只是英伟达掌⻔⼈，更像是全球  AI 供应链的 “ 定海神针 ”，每逢 AI 泡沫的质
疑此起彼伏，他总能镇住场⼦，给市场信⼼再添⼀把⽕。
如今，随着核⼼⼈物的突然退场，最先崩盘或许并⾮技术叙事，也不是算⼒需求，⽽是⽀撑起
这万亿投资狂潮的底层默契：上下游互相垫资、彼此兜底的好⽇⼦，到头了。
从台积电卡账期，到戴尔们临交付加价  45% 、英伟达撤融资 —— 没了核⼼担保⼈，谁都不愿接
最后⼀棒的 “ 击⿎传花 ”。⼀夜之间，产业链正从 “ 拼命加杠杆 ” ，迅速切回 “ 现款现货 ” 。
合同变废纸  宁吃官司也要涨价 45%
英国量化交易巨头  XTX Markets ⼀纸诉状，把服务器⼚商戴尔告上了法庭。

法庭⽂件披露了⼀笔原本已经板上钉钉的交易：双⽅此前签下了⼀份总额  5.49 亿美元的固定价
格服务器采购合同。为了防⽌中途⽣变， XTX 还专⻔多掏了  500 万美元，⽤来 “确保价格确定
性” 。
但临近交货，戴尔⽅⾯通知  XTX ，服务器零部件涨价太猛，公司 “ ⽆法⾃⾏消化这部分成本 ” ，
XTX 必须⾄少再多付  6200 万⾄  7000 万美元，才能拿到这批机器。
XTX 在诉状中表⽰，对戴尔推翻约定价格的做法 “ 感到震惊 ” 。但戴尔的态度同样直接 —— 据法庭
⽂件记录，戴尔⾼管当时告诉  XTX ，“ 其他⾯临涨价的公司也去找过别的供应商，但最后还是回
到了戴尔，接受了调价。 ”
放在当前的  AI 算⼒硬件市场上，这种卖⽅强势主导、甚⾄临交付强⾏改价的戏码，早已不是戴
尔⼀家。
据英国《⾦融时报》援引多位知情⼈⼠报道，戴尔、 HPE （慧与，从惠普分拆⽽来）、联想和超
微电脑（ Supermicro ）等服务器供应商，普遍在与客⼾签订英伟达  AI 服务器采购合同数⽉后、

设备即将交付时，要求⼤幅加价。部分客⼾收到的涨价幅度达到  25% 、 30% ，甚⾄  45%。
细分来看， GPU 服务器的实际交付价⾄少上涨了  20% 到  30%；⽽内存成本占⽐极⾼的  CPU
节点，价格直接翻了三倍以上。
“ 当你突然收到  25% 、 30% 甚⾄  45% 的涨价通知时，那种感觉⾮常令⼈不安。 ” ⼀位供应链知情
⼈⼠说道，现在提前⼏个⽉敲定的报价根本就不靠谱，因为它和设备到⼿时的实际成交价，差
额⼤得离谱。
另⼀位受访者则直接点破了现在的潜规则： “ 现在的常态是，我们签的合同价格其实只是⼀个参
考价（ indicative price ），等到交货时，价格可能被任意抬⾼。 ”
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这轮涨价砸向的，并不是缺乏议价能⼒的⼩买家。据悉，受冲击的包括全球最头部的⼏家⾦融
与量化交易机构——Jane Street 、 Optiver 和  Hudson River Trading ( 简写  HRT ）。在超⼤云
⼚商（ Hyperscalers ）之外，这群靠算法直接从市场变现的机构，是  AI 算⼒硬件最⼤的采购
⽅。 HRT 甚⾄在挪威⼀座废弃地下矿井⾥改造了⼤型数据中⼼， XTX Markets 则在芬兰⾃建数
据中⼼。
HRT 位于  Lefdal 矿场数据中⼼深处的⼀条通往⼭内的道路
位于芬兰卡亚尼森林中的  XTX 数据中⼼
⾯对供应商临⻔⼀脚的加价，⼤多数交易机构最终还是选择了吞下账单。⼀位知情⼈⼠坦⾔：
“ 我们从未说过不（ We’ve never said no ）。说到底，算⼒对我们太重要了，我们只能付钱。 ”
⾄于服务器⼚商宁愿撕破脸、甚⾄吃官司也要强⾏调价的直接原因，摩根⼤通（ JPMorgan ）的
⼀组研究数据给出了答案：⾃  2024 年初以来，内存（ Memory ）成本已经暴涨超过  400% 。  服

务器整机原本就是低⽑利⽣意，上游元器件价格失控，⼚商按原价履约就意味着直接流⾎。
上游同步收紧  台积电开始要现⾦

### Page 3

下游服务器⼤⼚在临交付时把成本硬塞回给买家，⽽⼏乎在同⼀时间段，产业链最上游的晶圆
⼚和芯⽚巨头，也在收紧钱袋⼦。
就在⻩仁勋离开英伟达⼀周后，⻓期作为英伟达核⼼代⼯伙伴的台积电，传出了调整付款条件
的消息。
据台媒报道，随着业内对  AI ⾏业 “ 循环融资 ” 的争议不断升温，台积电正全⾯加强客⼾信⽤管理
—— 根据企业的资产负债表、历史损失率、市场竞争⼒及经营前景重新核定信⽤额度。超出额
度的芯⽚订单，必须额外⽀付现⾦；部分客⼾甚⾄必须提前⽀付预付款，才能锁定晶圆产能。
对  AI 芯⽚设计公司和定制芯⽚（ ASIC ）⼚商来说，这道⼝⼦⼀旦收紧，现⾦流⽴刻吃紧。
我们知道，芯⽚制造的资⾦周转周期原本就很⻓：晶圆在台积电制造完成后就需要结清货款，
但芯⽚随后还要经过先进封装、测试，交付给终端客⼾后才能回款。台积电卡住信⽤额度，意
味着订单规模越⼤，芯⽚设计企业⾃⼰需要垫付的资⾦就越多。
压⼒已经直接体现在芯⽚⼚⾝上。
第三季度，联发科、创意电⼦、世芯电⼦等  ASIC ⼚商密集启动筹资，通过发⾏可转债、增资或

银⾏贷款补充现⾦。其中，联发科在  8 ⽉底完成了  39 亿美元的可转债定价，英伟达⼀家就认购
了其中的  35 亿美元。
即便是联发科这个体量的芯⽚巨头，在超⼤额  AI 订单的垫资压⼒⾯前，也不得不借助资本市场
和核⼼伙伴的资⾦托底。
5000 亿美元融资落空？英伟达更不想兜底

### Page 4

⽐台积电收紧账期更值得关注的，是⻛暴眼中⼼的英伟达⾃⾝的变化。
过去两年，英伟达深度参与了全球  AI 基础设施的扩张，它不仅是卖卡的供应商，更通过直接投
资  AI 初创公司、为数据中⼼项⽬牵线融资、甚⾄提供算⼒回购担保等⽅式，⼀⼿把整个算⼒基
建的盘⼦推到了前所未有的⾼度。
但多⽅权威信源显⽰，这套激进的玩法正在踩下刹⻋ —— ⽽且转向在⻩仁勋正式离职前就已开
始。
据《华尔街⽇报》披露，⻩仁勋离职前⼣，英伟达就已经开始收紧对  AI 企业的资⾦⽀持，要求
部分云服务合作伙伴必须先拿到终端客⼾的预付款，以降低英伟达⾃⾝承担的融资⻛险。
上 周 ，《 华 尔 街 ⽇ 报 》 进 ⼀ 步 曝 出 ⼀ 个 更 具 ⻛ 向 标 的 细 节 ： 英 伟 达 此 前 联 合   A p o l l o 、
Blackstone （⿊⽯）、 BlackRock （⻉莱德）、 Brookfield 、 Goldman Sachs （⾼盛）和
KKR  等华尔街顶级资管与投⾏机构推动的  5000 亿美元  AI 基础设施融资计划，截⾄发稿前并未

实际部署资⾦。
与此同时，英伟达⾃⼰账⾯上背负的隐性⻛险，也开始浮出⽔⾯。
路透社分析指出，英伟达此前已承诺在⼀定条件下，购买部分云服务商未能售出的算⼒容量，
相关承诺规模⾼达  360 亿美元。在算⼒供不应求的狂热期，这类 “ 保底回购 ” 安排是极佳的杠杆⼯
具，能帮新兴云服务商顺利从银⾏和资本市场拿到贷款；但⼀旦终端算⼒需求出现波动或不及
预期，这  360 亿美元就会变成实打实的敞⼝，直接压在英伟达⾃⼰的资产负债表上。
对于刚刚接棒的英伟达新管理层⽽⾔，如何稳妥拆解和管理这类历史承诺带来的⻛险，已经不
再是纸⾯推演，⽽是⼀道必须⽴刻作答的现实考题。
英伟达正在从过去的 “ ⼴撒⽹式扩张 ” ，转向更严格的合作伙伴筛选：能够证明真实终端客⼾需
求、⼿握⻓期订单和客⼾预付款的企业，依然有机会拿到英伟达的资⾦与产能⽀持；⽽那些⾼
度依赖外部输⾎、讲着宏⼤故事却缺乏确定收⼊来源的项⽬，接下来⾯对的⻔槛只会越来越
⾼。
“ 击⿎传花 ” 停摆  没⼈愿接最后⼀棒
从戴尔们的坐地起价，到台积电催现款、英伟达撤融资，满是防备  AI 泡沫破裂的寒意，似乎随

着⽼⻩的下台，全产业链都开始加速隔离⾃⾝的泡沫⻛险。
过去两年， AI 算⼒基建能以极快的速度铺开，靠的不只是芯⽚算⼒的迭代，还有⼀条互相托底
的信⽤链条 ——英伟达拿钱投资并做信⽤背书，云⼚和初创公司借钱买卡，服务器⼚商为了抢
份额签下固定价⻓约，台积电给出账期全⼒扩产。
⼤家都在押注⾼增⻓，所以彼此愿意扛⼀部分波动。但现在，这套默契正在⽡解。
有媒体统计，随着  AI 交易热潮降温，数据中⼼建设概念股的市值已经蒸发了  4.2 万亿美元。叠
加循环融资的质疑不断发酵，以及⻩仁勋离职带来的核⼼预期变动，供应链上每个理性的玩
家，都开始重新算账。
⻩仁勋的离开并不是这些⽭盾的起点，但它加速了⾏业⼼理防线的切换。当那个曾经⽤个⼈号
召⼒和公司账⾯为全⾏业兜底的⻆⾊离场，各环节对 “ 谁来买单 ” 的警惕⼼⽴刻拉满。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

但市场规则已经⽣变：买服务器没了固定价，找台积电流⽚要现款现货。往后没什么悬念，⼿
⾥有真⾦⽩银、能⾃⼰赚钱的买家继续玩；⽽那些全靠融资、账期和供应商垫钱撑⻔⾯的玩

## Document 282: ANT's IPO Is Hot, But Stock Market Gets Cold Feet And Gold Panic Worsens

Source PDF: all report pdf/ANT's IPO Is Hot, But Stock Market Gets Cold Feet And Gold Panic Worsens.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ANT's IPO Is Hot, But Stock Market Gets Cold Feet And Gold Panic Worsens
Date:    17 minutes ago
ANT's IPO Is Hot, But Stock Market Gets Cold Feet And Gold Panic Worsens

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ANT's IPO Is Hot, But Stock
Market Gets Cold Feet And Gold
Panic Worsens
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 19 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Gary Meulemans / Unsplash

I. Anthropic Makes History, Stocks Fall
Today marked Anthropic's stock market debut. It showed that enthusiasm
for AI remains strong, even as broader market sentiment has soured.
Anthropic raised $81.3 billion through its IPO, surpassing SpaceX's $75
billion offering to become the largest IPO in history. Including a concurrent

### Page 2

private placement, the deal raised $107.3 billion. The shares were priced
at $83, valuing Anthropic at $1.5 trillion and making it the world's 12th
most valuable publicly traded company. They opened at $111.50, briefly
pushing its valuation above $2 trillion, before retreating from an intraday
high of $115.10 to $95.05, still 14.5% above the IPO price.
The S&P 500 reversed an early rally this morning to fall 2.0%, while the
Nasdaq dropped 2.2% (charts). Not even the largest IPO in history could

reverse the jitters surrounding the AI trade.
The selloff extended to semiconductor stocks, with AMD plunging 7.7%
after a short-seller report raised concerns about its ties to Chinese
entities, including a US-blacklisted server manufacturer (chart). Coming
on the back of the Nvidia controversy and tighter US export controls, the

### Page 3

report provided another reminder of the political speed bumps facing the
AI trade.
For us, however, the IPO's biggest significance lies ahead. We have
argued that financial, political, and physical constraints may slow the AI
buildout, but are unlikely to halt it as long as demand for compute keeps
growing. Anthropic's future earnings reports will provide a clearer window
into AI adoption across the economy, helping us assess whether demand
for compute is strong enough to sustain one of the US economy's most

important stimulus programs: the AI buildout. We believe it is and continue
to view the recent selloff as a buying opportunity.
We are sticking with our S&P 500 target of 7,900, based on projected
2027 EPS of $425 and a forward P/E of 18.6. Industry analysts continue
to raise earnings estimates, with the consensus 2028 EPS estimate at
$496 versus our $450 forecast (chart). We suspect some irrational
exuberance, as these forecasts fail to account for the AI buildout's speed

bumps.

### Page 4

Investors, meanwhile, are increasingly skeptical of the analysts' optimistic
forecasts. So investors pushed the S&P 500 forward P/E down to 17.6
(chart). That's below last year's Liberation Day low. We think that panic
selling should abate soon.
Today's latest stock market selloff suggests that the two Bull/Bear Ratios
we track will continue to fall below their averages over time (since 1987)
this week (chart). From a contrarian perspective, that would be bullish.

### Page 5

II. US Capital Flows Supporting The Dollar
The latest Treasury International Capital (TIC) data show that foreign
demand for US financial assets remained exceptionally strong through
September. Net foreign capital inflows totaled $1.43 trillion over the 12
months through September, remaining near the record-high $1.82 trillion
last year (chart).
Equities attracted a staggering $944.7 billion, including $787.1 billion from
private foreign investors and $157.6 billion from foreign official institutions

(chart).

### Page 6

The 12-month total for foreign equity inflows has surged over the past two
years to around $1.0 trillion on a 12-month basis (chart).
Private foreign purchases of US equities reached $787.1 billion, nearly
matching the $821.5 billion pouring into US bonds (chart).

### Page 7

Interestingly, corporate bonds attracted more foreign private capital than
Treasuries, underscoring the appeal of US corporate debt (chart).
III. US Economy On A Roll
The robust foreign demand for US financial assets is consistent with the
underlying strength of the US economy. The Atlanta Fed's GDPNow
model estimates Q3 real GDP growth at a solid 3.2% (saar). Consumer
spending is expected to grow at a robust 2.5% pace, while business
investment is projected to surge 9.2% (chart).

### Page 8

The labor market remains strong too, with initial jobless claims at just
203,000 and continuing claims at 1.76 million, both near multi-year lows
(chart).
IV. Gold Keeps Falling Through Support Levels
The gold market is experiencing a selling panic. The recent selloff below
$4,000 per ounce, down to $3,682, was accompanied by record increases
in daily volume in the GLD ETF (charts). We recently pushed our $5,000
target from the end of this year to the end of next year. Our conviction

level has been greatly reduced by the selloff since the start of the Middle
East war this year. We expect that the gold price will find support around
$3,400.

### Page 9

💡

leave comments or questions at the end of our QuickTakes, and it
may be featured in the next "Just Ask Dr. Ed!"

## Document 287: Asia Outlook 2027_ Growth broadens, capital gets selective

Source PDF: all report pdf/Asia Outlook 2027_ Growth broadens, capital gets selective.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 25
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

After a stronger-than-expected 2026, Asian growth should
broaden in 2027 as AI tailwinds moderate, lower energy
prices support demand and supply-chain diversiﬁcation
continues. Capital ﬂows will become more selective as global
ﬁnancial conditions tighten. We favor the KRW, TWD, SGD, and
expect Australia and Philippines to lead easing moves
Deepali Bhargava
How did Asia’s economy perform in 2026,
and what will look different in 2027?
2026 was stronger than expected

Asia surprised on the upside in 2026, with regional GDP growth
outperforming expectations, led by strong exports and investment
activity. Investment remained robust across much of the region,
supported by technology-related spending and government-led
infrastructure projects. This enabled export-oriented economies
such as South Korea, Taiwan and Singapore to signiﬁcantly exceed
consensus growth forecasts.
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

The picture was less favourable in economies more reliant on
domestic demand. Indonesia and the Philippines disappointed
expectations as household consumption remained subdued.
Higher energy prices weighed on real incomes, while the Philippines
also faced a drag from slower government spending amid political
and corruption-related controversies. Both economies attracted
relatively less tech-related foreign direct investment than the
region's manufacturing and semiconductor hubs, limiting support

from global dynamics.
India was a notable outperformer. Despite beneﬁting less directly
from the AI-driven boom and facing higher energy import costs, it
expanded by a robust 8.2% year-on-year in the ﬁrst half of 2026.
Growth continued to be supported by structural reforms, public
capital expenditure and subsidies that cushioned households from
higher fuel prices.
AI-led divergences should narrow in 2027, favouring South Asia
Overall, we expect GDP growth excluding China to slow to 3.8% in

2027, down from 4.5% in 2026. Tight chip supply should continue
to support investment growth through 2027, but as capex growth
slows from today's exceptional pace, AI's contribution to growth is
likely to moderate.
As AI tailwinds fade and the full eﬀects of monetary tightening are
felt, growth in developed Asia, particularly Korea and Australia,
could slow more than in developing Asia. Korea is the clearest
example, where growth has been concentrated in technology-

related sectors while domestic demand, non-tech industries and
broader wage growth have lagged. As in previous investment-led
booms, the beneﬁts have been slower to ﬁlter through to the wider
economy.
By contrast, the Philippines’ 2026 growth disappointment was
driven primarily by policy and political uncertainty, both of which
are likely to prove temporary. India continues to occupy a relative
sweet spot. While it beneﬁted less from the AI boom, it’s also less

exposed to any correction. Structural reforms, infrastructure

The divergence is less pronounced in parts of Southeast Asia,

Trade growth likely to moderate in 2027
Asian economies are expected to be the largest contributors to
global trade volume growth for the third consecutive year in 2026.

According to World Trade Organisation forecasts, global trade
growth is expected to moderate in 2027 as AI-related demand
cools from exceptionally strong levels, though the deceleration
should be gradual rather than abrupt.
Recent data has been mixed. Some signs of moderation appear in
semiconductor export growth in Japan and Taiwan, and export
orders softened across the board in October. However,
semiconductor exports from Korea and China continue to post

strong gains. Data from Taiwan suggest it’s still too early to call a
turning point.
Even so, trade growth should remain relatively resilient in 2027,
supported by ongoing supply-chain diversiﬁcation, continued
investment ﬂows and still-elevated levels of AI-related activity. This
is even as the pace of expansion normalises.
Pockets of domestic demand strength should support growth
The structural backdrop for consumption in Japan is becoming
more supportive. Wage growth has increased signiﬁcantly since the

start of the year. Also, households are gradually shifting away from
the cash-heavy behaviour associated with the deﬂation era
towards greater participation in ﬁnancial markets. This supports
wealth creation and consumption.
Singapore should continue to see resilient consumption growth,
supported by strong FDI inﬂows and spillovers from the AI
investment cycle. These tailwinds are feeding into ﬁrm wage
growth and stronger household purchasing power. Singapore has

How is AI reshaping Asia's growth story,
and can the region continue to beneﬁt as
the boom matures?
Looking ahead to 2027, North Asia should continue to dominate AI
trade. Southeast Asia should play an increasingly important role as
its AI story is driven by production, investment and supply-chain

integration rather than AI adoption itself. While the region doesn’t
have the largest AI companies, it’s become indispensable to
hardware and manufacturing ecosystems that make AI possible.
As ﬁrms diversify supply chains and invest in AI infrastructure,
Southeast Asia stands out as one of the largest structural
beneﬁciaries of the AI boom outside North Asia.

### Page 5

Trade dominance: Asia accounted for about 77% of AI-driven
exports in 2025
Asia sits at the centre of the global AI hardware ecosystem. It
dominates global trade in AI-enabling goods. East Asia, excluding
China, accounts for more than 40% of global exports of AI-related
products. Southeast Asia contributes another quarter despite
having a much smaller share of overall world trade. With China
contributing another 13%, Asia is generating much of the world's

growth in AI-related exports.
The same dynamic is visible on the import side. AI-enabling goods
account for a far larger share of imports than East Asia’s and
Southeast Asia’s share of overall trade, underscoring the deep
integration of global AI supply chains.
Growth in the ﬁrst half of 2026 reinforced Asia's leadership, with the
region's largest AI exporters recording the fastest growth. In the
second quarter, exports of AI-enabling goods surged by 80% YoY.

East Asia’s contribution to global export growth doubled to 32.3
percentage points in just six months, while Southeast Asia’s share
similarly surged to 22.0 percentage points. China alone accounted
for a further 12.8 percentage points, underscoring the region’s
outsized role in driving global trade.
These trade relationships have shifted markedly over the last
decade. One notable change is Southeast Asia's growing share of
US imports, consistent with the rapid build-out of data centres by

hyperscaler ﬁrms. Yet Southeast Asia has lagged in strengthening
trade linkages with East Asia, excluding China, and within the
region itself. This suggests there’s scope for deeper regional
integration as the AI ecosystem expands.
Broadening beneﬁciaries: Asia’s AI story extends beyond
Korea/Taiwan
On products, much of the focus has been on memory chip exports
from Korea and Taiwan. But the beneﬁciaries of the AI boom are
increasingly broadening. ASEAN economies have attracted

substantial foreign direct investment in semiconductors ucand

increased their share of global non-memory chip production.
Singapore and Malaysia now account for more than 33% of global
ampliﬁer chip exports, up from 23% a decade ago. The Philippines
and Thailand have made impressive gains, together accounting for
around 2.5% of global exports.
Looking across the AI value chain, almost all AI-enabling goods
trade is concentrated in intermediate inputs (62%) and equipment
(37%). While East Asia, ex-China, remains the dominant supplier at

every stage of the value chain, Southeast Asia has emerged as a
critical second hub. It accounts for roughly a quarter of global
exports of AI-related equipment, spanning the full value chain from
semiconductor manufacturing to the servers and other
infrastructure deployed in data centres. This is particularly
signiﬁcant because the fastest-growing areas of AI investment are
in physical infrastructure, including data centres, servers, and
semiconductor packaging and testing, where Southeast Asia has

built a strong competitive advantage.
Southeast Asia's importance is especially evident in the middle of
the value chain. Together, East Asia and Southeast Asia account for
nearly 90% of global trade in AI-related intermediate inputs, with
Southeast Asia contributing around 23%.
While growth in AI-related demand may eventually moderate,
Asia's central role in hardware and manufacturing ecosystems
should help sustain investment, trade and industrial activity. This

should keep the region a key beneﬁciary of the AI cycle for years to
come.

Is AI driving inﬂation or productivity in
Asia? And is it disrupting jobs or
transforming them?
AI's economic impact in Asia is likely to be uneven over time. Signs
of AI-related inﬂation are already visible, particularly in Korea,
where technology-related price increases are contributing
meaningfully to headline CPI. By contrast, evidence of productivity

improvements remains concentrated in parts of the technology
sector and highly AI-complementary occupations. While the
productivity beneﬁts could ultimately prove more signiﬁcant,
they’re likely to emerge gradually and take longer to be reﬂected in
economy-wide data.
AI is starting to show up in Asia's inﬂation data, especially Korea
AI is likely to be inﬂationary in the short term. This is reﬂected in the
sharp rise in memory chip prices and strong tech exports, as

demand for chips continues to outpace supply. Recent consumer
price data suggest electronics prices are beginning to recover
across parts of Asia, with the strongest increases seen in mobile
phones, computers and communication equipment.
The sharpest increases have been in Korea, where data storage
device prices rose around 40% YoY year-to-date. Inﬂation is
accelerating rapidly in these categories. Mobile phone prices rose
11% YoY in October, up from just 2% at the start of the year.

Computer-related prices accelerated to 24% YoY from 6% earlier in

the year. Mobile handset and communication device inﬂation has

Contribution to headline inﬂation varies across Asia, but generally

In most other Asian economies, the contribution remains below 10

Evidence of AI-related inﬂation in services is less clear-cut.
Information and communication services inﬂation has picked up in
Korea and Malaysia, while falling communication charges continue
to weigh on technology-related CPI components in Singapore.
Overall, however, the contribution to headline inﬂation remains
modest.
Emerging productivity gains
On productivity, the distinction between AI producers and AI users
matters. The IMF ﬁnds that advanced Asian economies like

Singapore, Korea and Japan have a relatively high concentration of
jobs where AI is more likely to augment workers than replace them,
creating greater potential for productivity gains. Around 40% of
jobs in Singapore are estimated to be highly complementary to AI.
The key question, however, is whether these gains are already
visible in the data. Direct evidence remains limited given the short
time frame of AI adoption. Australia is one of the few economies in

the region where available data draw some tentative conclusions.
Our analysis of changes in real output per worker suggests
productivity growth has improved in parts of the technology sector,
particularly telecommunications services. While it’s still too early to
attribute this entirely to AI, the evidence is consistent with
technology adoption beginning to support productivity growth in
selected industries.
Early evidence points to adaptation, not job losses

Evidence from Korea suggests AI may be reshaping labour demand
unevenly. Youth employment has weakened in highly AI-exposed
industries, while employment among workers in their 50s has
increased. India and the Philippines are among the countries most
exposed to AI because of their sizeable outsourcing sectors. Early
evidence points to job transformation and augmentation rather
than widespread displacement.
In the Philippines, employment in information and

communications, professional services, and scientiﬁc and technical
services has grown by around 4.5% over the past year. Recent
readings remain close to long-term trends. Digitally delivered
services exports continue to expand, even as the composition
shifts. Financial services and computer services are increasingly
driving growth, while the share of more traditional business process
outsourcing activities becomes less dominant.
The evidence from India points in a similar direction. Rather than

widespread layoﬀs, AI's impact appears to be showing up through
slower hiring and changing skill requirements. While AI is reducing
demand for some traditional roles, it’s creating demand for new
skills. Traditional IT ﬁrms are hiring more cautiously, but global
capability centres (GCCs) continue to expand and absorb higher-
skilled talent. As a result, India's business services exports have
more than doubled as a share of GDP since the pandemic. Even as

AI automates more routine coding and back-oﬃce tasks, demand
remains strong for higher-value activities such as software
development, analytics, AI integration and cybersecurity.

How are Asia's trade and investment
relationships shifting?
Diversiﬁcation, not deglobalisation
The key lesson from 2026 is that tariﬀs and geopolitical tensions
are reshaping trade patterns rather than materially reducing
volumes. Rather than exiting Asia, ﬁrms have responded by
diversifying production, sourcing, and investment across a wider
range of regional economies.

Tariﬀs remain an important risk, particularly if the Trump
administration refocuses on trade policy. Recent experience
suggests higher tariﬀs are more likely to shift where production
occurs than to reverse the broader diversiﬁcation trend. While
economies such as Vietnam and Thailand may face greater
scrutiny after gaining signiﬁcant US market share, tariﬀ
diﬀerentials with China are likely to remain large enough to keep
incentives for ﬁrms to maintain diversiﬁed regional supply chains.

### Page 11

Diversiﬁcation does not mean decoupling from China
Despite tariﬀs, Asia ex-China's trade surplus with the US has almost
doubled since the end of 2021, driven primarily by Taiwan, Vietnam
and Thailand. Over the same period, much of the rise in ASEAN’s
trade surplus with the US was mirrored by a corresponding decline
in China’s surplus, pointing to a reconﬁguration rather than a
reduction of regional trade ﬂows. The pattern suggests supply
chains are being reconﬁgured across Asia rather than shifting away

from the region altogether.
More importantly, production relocation has not meaningfully
reduced China's role in regional supply chains. While ﬁnal assembly
and exports to the US increasingly take place in economies such as
Vietnam and Thailand, a signiﬁcant share of intermediate inputs
still originates in China. This means supply chains are becoming
more geographically diversiﬁed but remain closely integrated with
China.
Europe is becoming a more important trade and investment

partner
Asia's trade and investment relationships are becoming more
diversiﬁed, with Europe playing an increasingly important role.
Trade ties have strengthened signiﬁcantly in recent years, and Asia
continues to gain export market share in Europe. Since 2024,
ASEAN exports to the EU have risen by around 30%. ASEAN now
exports more to Europe than Korea, Taiwan and Japan combined.
Looking ahead, a renewed push towards free-trade agreements
could provide further support. The proposed India-EU Free Trade

Agreement is particularly signiﬁcant, while a growing network of
agreements between ASEAN economies and European partners is
helping to lower barriers to trade and investment.
The investment relationship with Europe is strengthening even
faster. EU foreign direct investment into ASEAN surged to $31 billion
in 2021, overtaking the US as ASEAN's second-largest source of FDI.
More importantly, investment decisions are increasingly shaped by
strategic imperatives rather than cost competitiveness alone.

European ﬁrms increasingly view ASEAN not just as a

Has inﬂation peaked in Asia, and what
are the key risks to 2027?
Inﬂation outlook 2027: Lower headline gains but stickier core

pressures
Though it’s too early to call a peak in inﬂation in most of Asia, we
do expect prices to moderate in many parts of the region in 2027,
driven by easing energy and food prices. The largest declines
should occur in economies such as the Philippines and Singapore,
where recent inﬂation was driven primarily by fuel and food shocks.
Economies at the centre of the AI hardware ecosystem, particularly
Korea and Taiwan, may experience stickier core inﬂation as

technology-related price pressures continue to build.
While headline inﬂation should move lower, the balance of risks is
shifting from commodity-driven inﬂation towards technology-
driven inﬂation. This implies that while goods inﬂation may
moderate as energy and food pressures fade, core inﬂation could
remain stickier than expected. As a result, inﬂation may prove more

persistent than historical relationships with energy and food prices
alone would suggest.
Energy and food disinﬂation should drive the decline in inﬂation
Inﬂation has likely not yet peaked across most Asian economies,
but we expect it to do so in 4Q26-1Q27, with a clearer moderation
emerging from 2Q27. The key driver is energy prices.
We expect Brent crude to average around US$77/bbl in 2027,
around 14% below 2026 levels. While occasional US-Iran tensions

should keep some geopolitical risk premium in the market, we
expect a growing supply surplus to outweigh these concerns
provided there are no major disruptions to Persian Gulf exports. A
US-Iran agreement would present further downside risk to oil prices
and, by extension, inﬂation across Asia. Reﬁned fuel prices should
moderate year on year. Diesel prices could decline more sharply as
crack spreads normalise following the return of Russian supply,

although margins are likely to remain above historical averages.
Inﬂation should ease more noticeably from the second quarter of
2027, aided by favorable energy base eﬀects as oil prices were
unusually high a year earlier. Lower oil prices should also gradually
ﬁlter through to retail fuel costs, helping push headline inﬂation
lower across much of developing Asia.
The disinﬂationary impact should be most pronounced in oil-
importing economies such as the Philippines and Singapore, where

fuel has been a major driver of recent inﬂation pressures. In
Singapore, fuel prices accounted for almost 90% of the YoY increase
in headline CPI since February 2026. In Australia, transport costs
accounted for more than 100% of the increase in headline inﬂation,
oﬀset by weakness elsewhere in the CPI basket. In the Philippines,
fuel accounted for roughly 60% of the direct increase in inﬂation.
The overall impact is likely larger once second-round eﬀects

through transport, services and other fuel-sensitive categories are
taken into account. As lower oil prices feed through, these
economies should experience some of the largest declines in
inﬂation across the region.

Food should provide a second source of disinﬂation in 2027. As El

in recent years, supporting a gradual moderation in food price
inﬂation.

persist, second-round eﬀects could become more important
through services costs and wages in technology-intensive sectors.

Lower oil prices should help ease inﬂation,

Source: CEIC
Are food price risks from EL Niño behind
us?
Probably not. The current El Niño is expected to remain strong to
very strong through 1Q27, and there remains a meaningful risk that
weather-related food price pressures persist into next year.
That said, a severe El Niño does not automatically translate into
higher inﬂation. Historical experience is mixed. During both the

2015-16 and 2023-24 El Niño episodes, headline inﬂation across
most of Asia was little changed, with only limited evidence of a
broad-based inﬂation shock. Taiwan was a notable exception in
2015-16. A key reason was that other commodity prices,
particularly oil and fertiliser, remained relatively subdued and were
generally declining during both episodes.

### Page 15

The inﬂation impact of El Niño is likely to depend as much on
energy prices as on weather conditions themselves. A sustained
decline in oil prices would not only lower fuel inﬂation directly, but
also reduce transport, fertiliser and production costs, helping to
limit second-round eﬀects on food prices.
This matters particularly for economies such as the Philippines,
where food carries a large weight in the CPI basket. Our estimates
suggest a 20% decline in fuel prices could lower headline inﬂation

by around 100bp. Evidence from the current episode suggests a
mixed and less alarming impact across food commodities. While
rice prices have remained relatively contained in both the
Philippines and India, wheat and vegetable oil prices have risen
more noticeably. Importantly, the latter appear to reﬂect
geopolitical disruptions and broader supply constraints as much as
El Niño-related eﬀects.
Food price risks in 2027 nevertheless remain skewed to the upside.

Looking ahead, global wheat production is projected to decline by
around 3% in 2026/27, reﬂecting weaker harvests in major
producers including the US, Australia and Argentina. Rice output is
also expected to decline by 2% in 2026/27, the ﬁrst annual decline
in a decade, as El Niño weighs on harvests in major Asian
exporters. Nevertheless, energy prices are likely to remain the
dominant driver of the inﬂation outlook. Food inﬂation risks could
delay the pace of disinﬂation in some economies, but lower oil

prices should still be the primary force behind headline inﬂation
moderation across most of developing Asia in 2027.
El Nino risks persist, but the inﬂation impact
remains limited and uneven

What does all this mean for central
banks?
If we’re right about fuel prices correcting, most Asian central banks
should gain conﬁdence that inﬂation is moving sustainably back
towards target, opening the door to policy easing in the second half
of 2027 to support growth. Japan remains the key exception. Unlike
elsewhere in the region, its inﬂation is increasingly being driven by
stronger wage growth, labour shortages and ﬁrmer inﬂation

expectations. This should keep the Bank of Japan on a tightening
path for longer than the rest of the region.
Growth slowdown should strengthen the case for rate cuts in
some economies
Real policy rates are near the upper end of historical ranges across
much of Asia, with the notable exceptions of the Philippines and
Taiwan. High real rates matter because they increase borrowing
costs and tend to weigh on consumption, investment and credit
growth.

Growth is already slowing in several economies. Lagged eﬀects of
past monetary tightening could weigh on activity into 2027.
Despite still-negative real rates, the Philippines has seen growth
moderate following an aggressive tightening cycle, while Australia
is likely to face a softer growth backdrop. Higher Australian interest
rates have weighed on household demand and are beginning to
feed through to the housing market. Falling house prices there

could further constrain consumption through negative wealth
eﬀects, while weaker housing activity may weigh on investment

### Page 17

and government revenues. Australia’s labour market has also
started to show clearer signs of cooling.
Against this backdrop, Australia and the Philippines are the two
economies where we see the strongest case for rate cuts in the
second half of 2027 as policymakers shift focus from inﬂation to
supporting growth.
The case for easing is less compelling in North Asia
By contrast, growth in Korea and Taiwan is likely to moderate as the
AI-driven upswing matures and overheating concerns recede. Even

so, inﬂation should remain close to target, and economic activity
should remain relatively resilient. As a result, policy easing is less
urgent than in economies such as Australia and the Philippines.
FX considerations could delay easing in India and Indonesia
FX stability may limit the scope for rate cuts in India and Indonesia.
Unlike some regional peers, the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has
historically been reluctant to use interest rates to defend the rupee.

However, policymakers recently signalled that rates could help
support currency stability if needed. While India's growth and
inﬂation backdrop should balance better in 2027, with inﬂation
closer to target and growth moderating gradually, FX management
is an important wildcard. Given the relatively shallow hiking cycle,
we don’t expect the RBI to come under signiﬁcant pressure to
reverse course and cut rates.
Indonesia faces a diﬀerent challenge. The rupiah remains one of the

region's most interest-rate-sensitive currencies. We expect it to
remain among Asia’s weakest currencies in 2027. Even if growth
softens and inﬂation stays manageable, Bank Indonesia (BI) may
be more cautious about easing policy. Maintaining a suﬃciently
attractive rate diﬀerential and limiting FX volatility will likely remain
important policy considerations, suggesting BI may lag other
central banks in the easing cycle.

targets by 4Q27

Can tech-linked currencies maintain their
leadership in 2027?
FX outlook 2027: Rotation underway, but tech currencies should
continue to lead
Asian currencies have had a mixed run in 2026. AI-linked currencies
such as the KRW, CNY, SGD and MYR generally outperformed, while
the INR, IDR and PHP weakened amid softer growth, higher energy
prices and domestic challenges. One notable exception among the
tech-linked economies is the Taiwanese dollar, which depreciated

by around 5% despite strong current account balances, as a tech-
sector sell-oﬀ in recent months triggered equity outﬂows.
In 2027, lower oil prices and a softer dollar should provide a more
supportive backdrop for Asian FX. However, we think current
account balances and capital ﬂows are likely to play a larger role
than interest-rate diﬀerentials in driving FX performance. While
some rotation into South Asian currencies is already underway, we
continue to expect tech-linked currencies, particularly the SGD in

Southeast Asia and the KRW and TWD in North Asia, to outperform.
This is thanks to strong current account surpluses and exporter FX
conversion, which provide ongoing support. By contrast, currencies
with weaker external positions and less stable capital inﬂows
remain more vulnerable.
Current account surpluses trump rate diﬀerentials in 2027
Rate diﬀerentials are becoming less supportive for EM Asia. Most
Asian central banks are nearing the end of their tightening cycles

and are likely to prepare for policy easing during 2027. Interest-rate

support for Asian currencies should gradually diminish, particularly
for rate-sensitive currencies such as the IDR.
Instead, current account balances and capital ﬂows are likely to

been among the strongest performers since the US-Iran conﬂict in
February 2026, appreciating despite a relatively resilient DXY. This
suggests domestic fundamentals are playing a larger role in driving

We remain bullish on the KRW, SGD and TWD. The AI cycle may be
plateauing, but activity remains high. Strong AI-related exports
should continue to support current account surpluses, while
exporter FX conversion should help oﬀset capital outﬂows. In Korea,
sizeable equity outﬂows since September have not prevented KRW
appreciation, highlighting the importance of exporter conversion
and capital repatriation. Measures to encourage domestic
investment and improve shareholder returns could further support

the KRW in 2027. Similarly, Japan's eﬀorts to improve domestic
returns and shareholder distributions could gradually slow capital
outﬂows over time. So, currencies backed by large current account
surpluses, particularly the KRW, TWD and CNY, should be relatively
well supported even as rate diﬀerentials become less favourable.
The main risk to this view is a sharper-than-expected slowdown in
the AI cycle, which could weaken export earnings and reduce

current account support for these currencies.

Post-midterms, there’s been a rotation from North Asian to South

improved valuations across parts of South Asia. Falling energy
prices should continue to support oil-importing currencies such as
the INR, IDR and PHP.
However, tighter global ﬁnancial conditions mean capital will not be

distributed evenly. We remain more constructive on the INR, as
growth should remain relatively resilient, supported by supply-
chain diversiﬁcation and continued FDI inﬂows. By contrast,

PHP more vulnerable.
Broadly, real policy rates should remain supportive, though capital
ﬂows and external balances are likely to play a larger role. As
inﬂation eases, real policy rates should become increasingly
supportive for Asian currencies, particularly with central banks likely
to cut rates only gradually and with a lag. This should keep real
rates elevated through much of 2027, supporting FX. The largest

beneﬁciary of this is likely to be the PHP. Disinﬂation is expected to
outpace policy easing, resulting in a meaningful improvement in
real rate diﬀerentials. That said, real rates are unlikely to be the
dominant driver of FX performance, with current account dynamics
and capital ﬂows playing a larger role.
Most tech-linked FX have failed to fully
reﬂect strong current account surpluses
Note: * data for 3Q26
Source: Bloomberg, CEIC
Will resident demand for foreign assets

continue to constrain Asian FX?
Resident demand for foreign assets remains a key reason why
stronger external balances aren’t translating into sustained Asian
FX appreciation. Current account surpluses remain supportive for
several Asian currencies.
Two structural forces are at work. First, resident demand for foreign
assets continues to rise as retail and institutional investors, along
with corporates, seek greater diversiﬁcation and access to higher

Your privacy is important
